Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in crystals theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 909 and the invariant I(A,B) = 322 mod 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 909 + O(1/n^322). Summing over all modes and applying the crystals convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in crystals theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 545 and the invariant I(A,B) = 158 mod 1.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Theorem 220.4 (Invariance Principle)

Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
